{"type":"assistant","message":{"id":"gen-1783077914-fceRlXYyBQwbG3b5SkEg","type":"message","role":"assistant","container":null,"content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"Write_7","caller":{"type":"direct"},"name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/transform_proposal.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nTransform HWPX project proposal template by filling in placeholders from JSON data.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport zipfile\nimport re\nimport io\nfrom datetime import datetime\n\ndef load_json_data(path):\n    \"\"\"Load and return JSON data from file.\"\"\"\n    with open(path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        return json.load(f)\n\ndef normalize_budget(value):\n    \"\"\"\n    Normalize budget value: remove commas but keep currency symbol.\n    e.g., '₩1,200,000' -> '₩1200000'\n    \"\"\"\n    if not isinstance(value, str):\n        return value\n    # Keep the currency symbol (first character if it's not a digit)\n    # and remove all commas\n    result = value.replace(',', '')\n    return result\n\ndef calculate_months_from_range(date_range_text):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate number of months from a date range text.\n    Supports formats like:\n    - '2026-08 ~ 2026-10'\n    - '2026-10 ~ 2026-12'\n    - '2027-01'\n\n    Returns month count as integer, or None if cannot parse.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Pattern to match date ranges like \"2026-08 ~ 2026-10\" or \"2026-08 ~ 2026-08\"\n    range_pattern = r'(\\d{4})[.-](\\d{2})\\s*~\\s*(\\d{4})[.-](\\d{2})'\n    # Pattern to match single dates like \"2027-01\"\n    single_pattern = r'(\\d{4})[.-](\\d{2})$'\n\n    range_match = re.search(range_pattern, date_range_text)\n    if range_match:\n        start_year = int(range_match.group(1))\n        start_month = int(range_match.group(2))\n        end_year = int(range_match.group(3))\n        end_month = int(range_match.group(4))\n\n        # Calculate months: (end_year - start_year) * 12 + (end_month - start_month) + 1\n        months = (end_year - start_year) * 12 + (end_month - start_month) + 1\n        return max(1, months)  # At least 1 month\n\n    # Check for single month pattern (no range)\n    single_match = re.search(single_pattern, date_range_text.strip())\n    if single_match:\n        return 1\n\n    return None\n\ndef remove_linesegarray_from_paragraph(paragraph_xml):\n    \"\"\"\n    Remove all hp:linesegarray elements (case-insensitive) from a paragraph XML string.\n    This is critical for the document to render correctly.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Remove <hp:linesegarray /> and <hp:lineSegArray /> (self-closing)\n    result = re.sub(r'<hp:linesegarray\\s*/>', '', paragraph_xml, flags=re.IGNORECASE)\n    result = re.sub(r'<hp:lineSegArray\\s*/>', '', result, flags=re.IGNORECASE)\n\n    # Also handle cases with content between tags if they exist\n    result = re.sub(r'<hp:linesegarray>.*?</hp:linesegarray>', '', result, flags=re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL)\n    result = re.sub(r'<hp:lineSegArray>.*?</hp:lineSegArray>', '', result, flags=re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL)\n\n    return result\n\ndef process_xml_content(xml_content, data):\n    \"\"\"\n    Process XML content:\n    1. Replace placeholders {{key}} with values\n    2. Calculate and append month spans for phase lines\n    3. Remove linesegarray from modified paragraphs\n    \"\"\"\n    modified = False\n    result = xml_content\n\n    # Track which paragraphs contain placeholders or phase references\n    # We'll process the XML paragraph by paragraph\n\n    # Find all paragraphs\n    para_pattern = r'(<hp:p[^>]*>)(.*?)(</hp:p>)'\n\n    def process_paragraph(match):\n        nonlocal modified\n        open_tag = match.group(1)\n        content = match.group(2)\n        close_tag = match.group(3)\n\n        original_content = content\n        new_content = content\n\n        # Replace placeholders {{key}}\n        for key, value in data.items():\n            placeholder = f'{{{{{key}}}}}'\n            if placeholder in new_content:\n                # Normalize budget if this is the budget key\n                if key == '예산':\n                    value = normalize_budget(value)\n                new_content = new_content.replace(placeholder, str(value))\n\n        # Check if this is a phase line that needs month span calculation\n        # Look for patterns like \"1단계:\", \"2단계:\", \"3단계:\" followed by date content\n        phase_patterns = [\n            (r'1단계:[^<]*', '단계1'),\n            (r'2단계:[^<]*', '단계2'),\n            (r'3단계:[^<]*', '단계3'),\n        ]\n\n        for pattern, phase_key in phase_patterns:\n            if re.search(pattern, new_content):\n                # Get the phase value from data\n                phase_value = data.get(phase_key, '')\n                # Calculate months from the phase value\n                months = calculate_months_from_range(phase_value)\n                if months and f'({months}개월)' not in new_content:\n                    # Append month span before the closing </hp:t> tag\n                    # Find the last </hp:t> in the content\n                    if '</hp:t>' in new_content:\n                        # Insert month span before the last </hp:t>\n                        new_content = new_content.replace(\n                            '</hp:t>',\n                            f' ({months}개월)</hp:t>',\n                            1  # Only replace the first occurrence from the end\n                        )\n\n        # If content was modified, remove linesegarray\n        if new_content != original_content:\n            modified = True\n            new_content = remove_linesegarray_from_paragraph(new_content)\n\n        return open_tag + new_content + close_tag\n\n    # Process each paragraph\n    result = re.sub(para_pattern, process_paragraph, result, flags=re.DOTALL)\n\n    # Also do a global placeholder replacement for any remaining placeholders\n    # (in case they appear outside paragraphs)\n    for key, value in data.items():\n        placeholder = f'{{{{{key}}}}}'\n        if placeholder in result:\n            if key == '예산':\n                value = normalize_budget(value)\n            result = result.replace(placeholder, str(value))\n            modified = True\n\n    return result\n\ndef create_hwpx_output(template_path, output_path, data):\n    \"\"\"\n    Create HWPX output file by processing template and filling in data.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Read template as ZIP\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(template_path, 'r') as zin:\n        # Create output ZIP\n        with zipfile.ZipFile(output_path, 'w', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zout:\n            # Process each entry\n            entries = zin.namelist()\n\n            # Find mimetype entry if it exists\n            mimetype_entry = None\n            for entry in entries:\n                if entry.lower() == 'mimetype':\n                    mimetype_entry = entry\n                    break\n\n            # Write mimetype first (uncompressed) if it exists\n            if mimetype_entry:\n                mimetype_data = zin.read(mimetype_entry)\n                zout.writestr(zipfile.ZipInfo(mimetype_entry), mimetype_data, compress_type=zipfile.ZIP_STORED)\n\n            # Process and write all other entries\n            for entry in entries:\n                if entry == mimetype_entry:\n                    continue  # Already written\n\n                data_bytes = zin.read(entry)\n\n                # Check if this is an XML file that might contain placeholders\n                if entry.endswith('.xml') or entry.endswith('.XML'):\n                    try:\n                        xml_content = data_bytes.decode('utf-8')\n                        if '{{' in xml_content:\n                            # '2026-08 ~ 2026-10'\n    - '2026-10 ~ 2026-12'\n    - '2027-01'\n\n    Returns month count as integer, or None if cannot parse.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Pattern to match date ranges like \"2026-08 ~ 2026-10\" or \"2026-08 ~ 2026-08\"\n    range_pattern = r'(\\d{4})[.-](\\d{2})\\s*~\\s*(\\d{4})[.-](\\d{2})'\n    # Pattern to match single dates like \"2027-01\"\n    single_pattern = r'(\\d{4})[.-](\\d{2})$'\n\n    range_match = re.search(range_pattern, date_range_text)\n    if range_match:\n        start_year = int(range_match.group(1))\n        start_month = int(range_match.group(2))\n        end_year = int(range_match.group(3))\n        end_month = int(range_match.group(4))\n\n        # Calculate months: (end_year - Get the phase value from data\n                phase_value = data.get(phase_key, '')\n                # Calculate months from the phase value\n                months = calculate_months_from_range(phase_value)\n                if months and f'({months}개월)' not in new_content:\n                    # Append month span before the closing </hp:t> tag\n                    # Find the last </hp:t> in the content\n                    if '</hp:t>' in new_content:\n                        # Insert month span before the last </hp:t>\n                        new_content = new_content.replace(\n                            '</hp:t>',\n                            f' ({months}개월)</hp:t>',\n                            1  #
